<div class="section section--md">
<div class="container container--gap-top container--gap-sides">
<div class="grid grid--sm-1 grid--md-2 grid--gap">
<div class="note note--lg note--none">
<div class="note__icon">
<svg class="icon icon--primary" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<use xlink:href="/build/icons/spritemap.e9cf664e2c675497f0a9.svg#icon-sandbox" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"></use>
</svg>
</div>
<div class="note__body">
<div class="note__header">Sandbox</div>
<p>
                                    What you see is what you get: Sandbox allows you to create seamless worlds without any baking. Create your world and export to a game as you see it in the Editor.
                                </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="note note--lg note--none">
<div class="note__icon">
<svg class="icon icon--primary" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<use xlink:href="/build/icons/spritemap.e9cf664e2c675497f0a9.svg#icon-rendering" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"></use>
</svg>
</div>
<div class="note__body">
<div class="note__header">Rendering</div>
<p>
                                    Fully realtime dynamic global illumination with a fully PBR workflow allows you to create ultra realistic environments using the tools you're used to.
                                </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="note note--lg note--none">
<div class="note__icon">
<svg class="icon icon--primary" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<use xlink:href="/build/icons/spritemap.e9cf664e2c675497f0a9.svg#icon-physics" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"></use>
</svg>
</div>
<div class="note__body">
<div class="note__header">Physics</div>
<p>
                                    Physical simulations that take into account real world physical properties in a realistic manner to provide easily configurable and dynamic scenes.
                                </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="note note--lg note--none">
<div class="note__icon">
<svg class="icon icon--primary" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<use xlink:href="/build/icons/spritemap.e9cf664e2c675497f0a9.svg#icon-audio" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"></use>
</svg>
</div>
<div class="note__body">
<div class="note__header">Audio</div>
<p>
                                    Use CRYENGINE's exclusive Audio Translation Layer that allows you to choose the middleware you want, when you want. Allowing for workflows that work for you.
                                </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
